After a poll has seen the mount slewing, an unreadable poll sleeps and polls again, asking no slewing state beside a park state nobody can read, since "not slewing" there may be a park that arrived and "lost" would park into it; a later poll that reads parked answers "parked", one that reads not parked goes back to the slewing rule, and only the park's deadline answers "unconfirmed", the wait any park still reading slewing already gets. S5's case of an immediate stop is re-pinned as a deliberate pin change (`test_park_readback_unreadable_after_slewing.py`). **The pause no longer asks a stop an idle stop confirmed** (S7, #471). A pause that leaned on an idle stop asking on its own task reads the stop back at the first due time after that task ends and asks only if it is still not confirmed; a pause that never leaned on one asks as before, with no read in front (5.8). The run-start cooling wait, the camera-lane wait and a cloud hold's release gate also read the safety monitor now, and a pause they open ends their idle look (5.8, #452). A cloud hold, which keeps tracking on by design, runs its floor, flip and tracking checks on the hold's own clock (5.8, #203, #205). |
| 6.18 | Follower | A later target shot while the mosaic waits never keeps the cursor past `group_ready_ts` (1.6). |

## 8. Build plan

Every slice ships on its own, keeps every existing plan and flow byte-identical except where a fix is named, and names the mutant each new test kills. A test that still passes with its branch deleted is not a test. Subagents write code and never commit; the operator commits with explicit pathspecs. The issues in section 9 were filed on 2026-09-23 (#147 to #188, plus comments on #132, #136, #141 and #142), and the build is tracked in #189. Each slice closes the issues it names.

### S0: defects the mosaic stands on (no mosaic yet; ship at least one release before S3)

1. `flows/store.py`: FLOW_SCHEMA 3, the 23.4 migration with its note, future-schema refusal, visible rows for unreadable files, and the writer stamping 3. Palette default rotation -1 (`nodes.py:117`, `nodeDefs.ts:199`). The golden `server/tests/fixtures/flow_plan_golden/ngc7331_quick.json:71` is updated deliberately (23.4 becomes null).
2. `FlowGraph.validation_errors` refuses flow back-edges, with the same refusal in both drop resolvers. Correct the `compile.py:72-75` docstring.
3. `flowsConnect` replaces the existing wire only on **flow** inputs (`flowsSlice.ts:298-307`).
4. `plan_identity_errors` on all five start paths.
5. Fold `_target_complete` onto `_step_complete`.
6. The accepted-mode visit fix, in three parts (5.3).
7. The idle-clock park-hold in `_run_scheduled` and `_wait_until` (5.1): `_idle_since`, the three per-tick checks against the tracked target, and the latch. It also covers today's eta-0 and constraint waits.
8. `_setup_target` takes the tracking-refusal recovery's own centring result instead of the fabricated `centered: True` (`engine.py:2373`, 5.6 step 3).

Tests:

- A v2 file with 23.4 migrates, with the note; with 30 it does not. Mutant "migration keyed on `!= 23.4`" fails.
- A v9 file is listed as unreadable and is never loaded. Mutant "drop the refusal" loads it.
- A drawn loop is refused at save, at `/run`, and by both UIs' drop tests. Mutant "remove the check" compiles a zero-frame plan that the doctor calls clean.
- In a copy of the campaign example, both wires into `calib.do` survive a new wire, while a flow input is still replaced. Mutant "filter on any input" fails.
- Duplicate step ids get 422 on `/api/sequence/start` and `/run`, 409 on resume, and a refusal from recover and ResumeArm. Duplicate names are allowed with a warning when there are no groups and no named instructions. A stored session with duplicate names still appears in `load_all`. Mutant "model validator" makes it vanish.
- Accepted mode with a regraded frame: `_target_complete` and `_step_complete` agree.
- Accepted-mode cycle with injected rejects:
  - one always-rejecting filter among accepting ones: a visit ends after `per_visit` attempts, and the other filters' frames interleave at one reject per round. Today it is 10 rejects per round. Mutant "no increment in the quota branch" fails.
  - the same plan with `max_consecutive_rejects = 0`: no `NightQualityStop` while the other filters accept. Mutant "no increment" raises it after 20.
  - consecutive rejects span visits, and the step is set aside after `max_consecutive_rejects` of them. Mutant "local counter" never trips.
  - a pass of all rejects does not end the cycle; mutant "compare `_frames_done`" fails
- Idle-clock park-hold on the clocked simulator:
  - a setting target below its gate with the window open gets `set_tracking(False)` within `WAIT_TEARDOWN_S` plus one tick. Mutant "teardown keyed on `wait_ts - now`" keeps tracking.
  - a constraint wait park-holds the same way.
  - a tracked target that reaches its floor during a 60 s recheck loop is park-held at that tick.
  - the latch issues `set_tracking(False)` once per idle spell, not every 5 s.
- The tracking-refusal recovery that fails to converge leaves `result["centered"]` False. Mutant "fabricated True" fails.

### S1: identity, centring, hop hygiene, progress (useful to single targets on its own)

1. The new Target fields. `_setup_target` passes tolerance and attempts only when they are set (the call is otherwise identical).
2. Guider stand-down before every slew, after the issue's simulator test has demonstrated the defect. By ruling (5.6 step 2), only when `plan.guide` is set: a guider the operator started under a guiding-off plan is theirs.
3. `_pre_flip_side` keyed per target (with #136).
4. Dither counter reset on acquisition.
5. `_hop_focus_is_owed` (5.6 step 6): no age rule for hops; owed only on no good sweep tonight, a failed last sweep, `_refocus_due()`, or the group's first acquisition. `_post_flip_focus_is_owed` is unchanged.
6. Hop event cost and the ETA term.
7. `to_sequence_plan(flow_id=)`: deterministic ids for targets, steps and pool members.
8. `run_flow` CONTINUE, dormant sessions only (5.9): the factored plan replace plus `engine.start(session=)` inside one write-locked section that re-reads the session and never saves it separately; `accept_dropped`, `accept_recount`, `fresh`, and the ADOPT path for pre-S1 sessions. The skipped-panel exemption from `accept_dropped` was not built in S1, which has no groups. S2 built its server half: `TargetGroup.skipped_ids` (3.4), which `plan_replace_report` reads, so a step whose old target id is listed there is reported as skipped and never as dropped (5.9). S3 built the `skip` param that fills it (3.1; S3, #189): `parse_skip` reads it, and `_expand_mosaic` lists each skipped panel's id in its group's `skipped_ids`.
9. `GET /api/flows/{id}/progress` (`CAP_VIEW_STATUS`): per block, per panel, per step banked/owed from the flow's session as Run reads it (`current_for_flow`, 5.9), plus orphaned counts. The card chip for single targets.
10. The hub's `rotation_unavailable` flag.
11. An angle-check helper fed by `sky_angle`, not yet called by groups.

Tests:

- Two compiles of one flow give identical ids. An exposure change changes only that step id; a count change changes none. A different `flow_id` gives different ids. Mutant "uuid4" fails the continuity test.
- A second `/run` on the simulator banks on the same ledger entries. Dropping steps that hold frames gets 409 unless accepted. `fresh` starts a new session.
- Skipping a panel with banked frames and pressing CONTINUE does not 409, and re-enabling it restores its counts. Mutant "skipped panels count as dropped" 409s. S1 had no panels to skip; S2 built the case against a compile patched to skip one (S2, #189; `test_continue_skipped_panels.py`), and S3 built the end-to-end case with a real `skip` param (S3, #189; `test_flows_continue_skip_param.py`), which saves the flow with the panel skipped through `PUT /api/flows/{id}` and continues it on the real plan.
- A pre-S1 dormant session (uuid4 ids, frames banked) gets the 409 adopt answer, never a silent fresh start. ADOPT maps unique matches and lists the rest, and the `.bak` exists. Mutant "fresh on no shared ids" disarms it.
- A `counts` change against a session gets 409 with both totals unless `accept_recount`.
- CONTINUE and a ResumeArm start fired together (a barrier in the test, not a sleep) leave one run and a session file whose frames include every frame the winner banked. Mutant "save before start, outside the lock" loses frames.
- `goto_and_center` receives `0.5/60` when the target says 0.5 arcmin, and exactly today's arguments when unset.
- The guider stop is observed before the slew.
- Focus at a hop, on the clocked simulator: skipped 35 min after a good sweep with the temperature delta armed and unmoved; run after a temperature move; run on the group's first acquisition; run after a failed sweep. Mutant "30-minute age rule" sweeps at the 35-minute hop. Mutant "always sweep" fails.
- Re-acquiring target A in the lead window after target B keeps A's record. Mutant "engine-wide slot" fails.
- The first frame after an acquisition spends no dither.
- A viewer can read the progress route, and it carries no site-derived field.

### S2: the engine group driver (the core ultracode task; demonstrable through `/api/sequence/start` with no UI)

1. `TargetGroup` and `SequencePlan.groups`. Membership, eligibility, requeue and pass bookkeeping in `_run_scheduled`. `VisitBound` in `_run_steps`.
2. `sequence/panel_order.py`.
3. The non-raising reachability verdict at selection, tagged `wait` or `refuse`. Meridian hysteresis on `_group_flip_margin_s()` with post-hop side verification, and the visit deadline at the flip point.
4. `PanelDeferred`; set-aside semantics, persisted in `Session.set_aside`; the group anti-spin rule; the per-panel reject rule; the deferral wait; the angle check with its no-measurement cases; deferral on `rotation_skipped` and `rotation_unavailable`.
5. `on_target_complete` gated on panel completion. `after_group` gating. Bounded follower visits (`group_ready_ts`, 1.6).
6. Published group state. ResumeArm using the order function and passing rotation.
7. The hub rotate shortcut. FITS `MOSAIC` and `PANEL` keywords and a `$$PANEL$$` naming token (`naming.py:18-29`). The `types.ts` mirror.
8. ~~**S2b**: a classic Plan "cycle panels each pass" toggle.~~ Dropped by Revision 2, ruling 4: the Atlas and Sky door to the Plan is retired (#196), so no new Plan mosaics are made, and a `mosaic_group` with no `groups` entry keeps today's panel-first behaviour. Until S6 lands, the Sky copy is corrected to describe panel-first behaviour, as #154 suggests.

Tests (`server/tests/test_group_rotation.py`: the real `_run_scheduled` against the simulator hub on a clocked fake time, never sleeps, and doubles never read live device state):

- 2x2, L and R, count 3, `per_visit` 1. The visit order is exactly `1-1(L,R) 1-2 2-2 2-1`, repeated 3 times, in snake order. Every panel completes and `owed() == 0` completes the session. Mutant "remove the requeue" fails.
- `passes = 2` halves the hops. `visit_min_s` extends a visit at round boundaries.
- Seeded uneven progress: least complete goes first, and ties fall to never-visited, then snake.
- The floor with `on_floor = advance` on one panel sets only that panel aside.
- A forced centring failure on panel 3: deferred for 3 passes, then set aside with a warning alert naming it. Mutant "no failure counter" fails.
- A fixed-camera angle error beyond tolerance sets the group aside after the first measurement, and the alert carries both numbers.
- An all-deferred pass waits through `_wait_until`, with the safety gate called.
- A zero-exposure pass ends the group. Mutant "skip the group anti-spin" hits a wall-clock bound.
- A horizon-blocked panel: the number of selection iterations per simulated minute stays under a small bound. Mutant "return waiting after selection" (A's original) blows the bound.
- A meridian straddle on the clocked simulator gives exactly one pier change. A post-hop side mismatch defers. Mutant "no hysteresis" gives more than one change. This proves something only if the simulator mount picks its pier side from the hour angle as the AM5 does (`hub.py:6582-6585`); a test asserts that first.
- With the learned "cannot flip early" trait set, a panel 8 min before transit is not eligible. Mutant "use `_flip_lead_s`" makes it eligible.
- A visit whose next frame would cross its panel's flip point ends at the frame boundary before it. The measured pre-flip idle on the clocked simulator matches the A.4 formula within one frame.
- A follower selected during a meridian wait ends its visit at `group_ready_ts` and keeps its progress. The group then resumes. Mutant "follower runs to completion" never returns to the group.
- A panel that rejects every frame while the others accept is set aside after 3 visits. When every panel rejects, no panel is set aside by this rule. Mutant "count all zero-accept visits" sets the whole group aside in a cloud spell.
- A pier change with flips off sets that panel aside and the others keep shooting. No site with limits configured raises `SafetyAbort`. Mutant "every verdict waits" waits all night.
- A crash-resume the same night does not retry a set-aside panel, and the next night does.
- The no-measurement cases of 5.6 step 4 each have a case: fixed and verified shoots, fixed and never verified defers, rotate with a calibrated rotator warns.
- Guide-start failures (Revision 2, ruling 5), each under `guiding_action` warn, skip and abort in turn:
  - one panel fails its start: it is deferred to the next pass, the others shoot, it is retried after the others, and it is set aside after 3 consecutive failed passes with the alert naming it. No frame is shot on it unguided. Mutant "honour `guiding_action` per panel" shoots it unguided under warn, sets it aside at once under skip, and ends the run under abort.
  - a panel that fails twice and then starts guiding banks frames and resets its counter. Mutant "count lifetime failures" sets it aside later.
  - every attempted panel fails in one pass (at least two): the counters do not move and `guiding_action` decides (abort ends the run, skip sets the group aside, warn shoots unguided). Mutant "defer even then" spends three passes hopping a dead guider.
- Accepted mode with a rejecting grader terminates.
- A resume from a mid-group `done_map` picks the least complete panel, and ResumeArm re-centres on it with rotation.
- `on_target_complete` fires once per panel, at completion. Mutant "fire every visit" fails.
- `groups = []` is byte-identical against the golden plans. A `mosaic_group` with no group stays panel-first.

**As built** (S2, #189). Items 1 to 7 are built: the pure rules in `sequence/group_rules.py` and `sequence/panel_order.py`, the driver in the engine, and the hub, naming and model changes of item 7. The tests are split by rule rather than kept in one file, every engine case on the clocked simulator through `tests/_group_harness.py`: `test_group_rotation.py` (rotation, passes, order, set-aside, deferrals, the reject rule, guide starts, completion, published state, the ETA), `test_group_reach.py` (the reach verdict), `test_group_meridian.py` (the one pier change, the side check, the margin, the flip-point deadline, the pre-flip idle, a viewer across the wait), `test_group_followers.py` (followers and `after_group`), `test_group_angle_check.py` (the angle check), `test_group_set_aside_persisted.py` (set-aside across a restart) and `test_locked_angle.py` (Revision 2, ruling 9), with the pure rules in `test_group_rules.py` and `test_panel_order.py`. Not built: the guide-lost deferral of 5.6 step 7 (#303) and a follower filling a deferral wait (1.6, #304). The simulator mount reports the far pier side as soon as a tracked target crosses the meridian, with no slew (#298), so the straddle test counts the side each hop lands on, read at the moment it lands. Since S4 the simulator keeps the side its last move chose, as a German mount does (S4, #298; 5.7).

### S3: Flows vocabulary, compile, doctor, Tonight

1. TARGET params and ports, `create_params`, the `pass` outputs, loop-wire consumption, and the `_trigger_for` pass branch.
2. The compile entry (3.2), the `to_plan` expansion (3.3), the panel lane and `owner_of` (1.5), rig-fact injection, FLOW_SCHEMA 4 stamping.
3. Doctor R4, M1-M15 and L1 in the server. Correct the stale docstring at `doctor.py:13-17`; there is no UI copy. `LEGACY_TYPES` hides SLEW.
4. The wizard's mosaic kind, with a lane without SLEW and the loop wire. It is the generator behind "Send to Flow Wizard" (#196), whose stepped sheet lands in S4 with the modal and whose doors move in S6. The angle comes from the operator or USE MEASURED, never a default; the camera field comes from injected optics, and with none the wizard answers with a single target (1.8). The Examples drop SLEW, and an **eighth Example**, "M31 3x2, rotating", joins the acceptance corpus. It must load, validate and run on the simulator (`examples.py:3-7`).
5. The Tonight mosaic branch:
   - one `compute_night` per block
   - per-panel peak altitude through `_stamp_transit_alt` (gated)
   - `_budget` counting every panel, plus hops once measured (`tonight.py:510-555` dedupes them today)
   - a brief sentence
   - Campaign rows per panel from the progress route, with no pool required (`tonight.py:868-871` requires one today)
   - a TIMELINE band drawn from the worst and best panel (the `mosaicNightSummary` reduction)

Tests:

- A golden 2x2 compile and expansion.
- A two-lane flow with a mosaic scopes by wire. With no mosaic, all seven Examples compile byte-identically.
- The four worked cases of 1.5 compile as the table says. `owner_of` is a chain walk: a TARGET -> CYCLE -> DOME -> CAPTURE graph gives M13. Mutant "owner is any upstream TARGET" (the doctor's set walk) hands the CAPTURE to the mosaic.
- A pass wire from a mid-lane stage is M12. Appending a CAPTURE after the tail moves the loop wire to it.
- The wizard with no optics answers with a single target and a reason. It never writes a default angle; a grep test asserts no numeric angle literal in the mosaic kind.
- A v3 flow with no `counts` key compiles to attempts when loaded. Mutant "missing-key default Accepted" fails.
- Saving that flow writes `counts = Accepted subs` on every TARGET and POOL, and the save's answer says so (ruling 2). Mutant "switch on load" changes the compile of a flow nobody saved.
- A palette-created or wizard-created block gets Accepted subs and Any angle. An example keeps the angle it was written with (ruling 9): M31 stays at Rotate to PA 23.4.
- The anchor rule (ruling 3): a move of 9.9' on the 3x2 of 2.0 x 1.33 deg keeps the ids and 10.1' changes them; a 3.3 deg turn keeps them and 3.5 deg changes them; three saves of 6' each in the same direction re-anchor on the second, because each is measured against the anchor. Mutant "compare with the previous save" never re-anchors. Mutant "centres, not corners" keeps the ids of a 1x1 block turned 90 degrees.
- A mosaic flow stamps 4, and a single-target flow stamps 3.
- M1-M15 each have a positive and a negative case. M6 trips on a 4x1 (4 columns by 1 row, S4 orchestrator ruling 1) at 10% overlap at Dec 75 (38.9%) and not on a 3x3 at 25% at Dec 41 (3.1%).
- The loop wire emits no Instruction. A pass wire anywhere else says "will not run".
- The wizard option matrix passes the doctor.
- `nodeDefs` parity.
- Tonight calls `compute_night` once per block (spied). The budget equals panels times the per-panel figure. A viewer gets no altitudes.

**As built** (S3, #189). Items 1 to 5 are built. Item 1 is the vocabulary of 3.1, 3.7 and 1.3: `NodeDef.created_as` and `create_params`, through which a palette drop, the wizard and the Examples create a node, TARGET's params, the `pass` outputs and the `next` input, `LEGACY_TYPES`, and `_trigger_for`'s `pass` branch; the loop wire is consumed as the entry's `loop` and never emitted (1.4). Item 2 is the compile entry (3.2, `_target_entry`), the panel lane and `owner_of` (1.5), the expansion, the anchor a block's ids are keyed on and the save that writes it (3.3, `_expand_mosaic`, `reframe_carry`, `prepare_save`), the rig facts every route that compiles hands the compile, the doctor and the wizard (3.3, `_rig_facts`), and FLOW_SCHEMA 4 (3.6). Item 3 is the doctor of 1.8, in the server only, with its docstring corrected (#169) and SLEW hidden from the palette (1.7).

Item 4 is the `KIND_MOSAIC` kind of `flows/wizard.py`, "Mosaic", in `KINDS`. `generate_record` and `generate_answer` take the grid, the overlap, the angle and the rig facts by keyword, and `generate_answer` returns the notes that say why an answer is not what was asked. The camera field is `rig.fov_deg`, asked first: with none the answer is one target and `NO_OPTICS_REASON`, 1.8's sentence word for word, and the grid and the angle are not read. The angle is the operator's PA, folded into [0, 360), or the measured one under USE MEASURED (`measured_pa_deg`); with neither the answer is a refusal naming both ways, as it is for USE MEASURED with nothing measured, and "Rotate to PA" is refused when the rig says it has no rotator. The lane has no SLEW, and the loop wire leaves the tail the compile finds (`lane_tail`). A grid or an angle given with another kind is refused. A name is resolved through `tonight.resolve_target` (#190): a fixed catalogue row gets typed coordinates, a moving body stays a name the compile places at each run, and a name the catalogue does not know stays blank with a note, as no name does. The eighth Example is `example-m31-mosaic`, "M31 3x2, rotating": three columns by two rows at 25% overlap, a 1.346 x 0.9 deg field computed from a named sensor and focal length, Rotate to PA 55, and an LRGB cycle whose `pass` wire is the loop. It compiles to six panels in one rotating group and runs on the clocked simulator (`test_flows_examples_s3.py`), and the wizard's option matrix passes the doctor (`test_flows_wizard_mosaic.py`). Item 5 is `resolve_tonight` in `flows/tonight.py`. A multi-panel TARGET is one compiled entry and gets one `compute_night`, on its centre, and its row adds `mosaic` (`_mosaic_night`): the panels `compute_mosaic` lays out through `to_plan`'s own readings of the block, each shot panel's peak altitude from `framing._stamp_transit_alt` for the night, and `band`, the worst and the best panel by that altitude, the reduction `mosaicNightSummary` makes; a block whose panels cannot be placed says why and stamps none. The route is `CAP_VIEW_SITE_DERIVED`, so a viewer gets none of it. `_budget` multiplies a mosaic row's per-panel `goal_h` and `tonight_h` by its live panels, which the row names (`panels`), and adds `hop_h`: None until a measured hop cost is passed (`hop_cost_s`), never the engine's seed, and then the block's panels times the visits each takes (the engine's `_visits_owed` rule with nothing banked) at that cost, shared among the block's rows by shutter time. Since S4 a visit is the one the run makes, `max(passes, ceil(minVisit / pass))` passes (`readouts.visit_passes`, S4, #189), where S3 counted `passes` alone and so priced a block with a minimum visit at more hops than it makes, and the hops are shared among the rows drawn only, rounded so that they add up to the block's figure. `brief` gives each mosaic sentences for its grid, skips, overlap, angle, loop and hop, and says when the hop is not measured yet; S3 wrote the grid rows by columns, "2x3" for the Example's 3x2 (#339). Since S4 it writes the grid columns by rows (S4 orchestrator ruling 1), "M31 is a 3x2 mosaic of 6 panels", and where a panel label shares the sentence it says the size once in words (`grid_size`): "M31 is a mosaic of 3 columns by 2 rows shooting 5 of its 6 panels (panel 2-1 skipped, written row-column)". The visit it states is the run's too, read off the block's compiled entry, with a sentence when the minimum lengthens it, for the eighth Example at a 30 min minimum "A visit is 4 passes rather than the 1 asked, since it lasts at least its 30 min minimum and a pass takes 8 min." The eighth Example's tagline says what a cut night leaves, "the panels within a pass of each other" (S4, #189). Until S5 `brief` described one capture stage, the first FILTER CYCLE or, with none, the first CAPTURE, so a lane's later capture stages went unnamed while the visit sentence priced the pass out of every step the block owns (`_pass_s`): a rotating 2x2 whose lane is a CYCLE of Ha then a CAPTURE of OIII named no OIII and said a pass takes 10 min. The rule was older than S4, which made it contradict the visit sentence (#395). Since S5 `brief` names every capture stage, in the order the flow runs them (`flow_order`), a later one opening "It then", so that 2x2 reads "It then captures OIII 300 s × 2 (gain 100, bin 1)." after its cycle, and a cycle's sentence says how many subs each filter takes a pass, "3 subs per filter per pass", where it said "one sub" whatever `perCycle` held (S5, #395). Read as one chain, a flow's stages all read as the first block's where they go to more than one: a rotating 2x2 M31 whose lane is a CAPTURE of Ha, beside a TARGET M33 whose lane is a CAPTURE of L, named 360 s of stages under the 5 min pass its visit sentence states, and never named M33 (#470). Since the S5 integration each lane's first stage names the blocks the compile shoots it for, by the compile's own scoping rule (`_stage_sentences`, `_receivers`), "For M33 it captures L 60 s × 5 (gain 100, bin 1).", a stage no block holds says it shoots nothing, and a flow whose stages all go to one block reads as before (S5, #470); #470's other two halves, older than S5, were open until S7: a TARGET after the first earned no sentence of its own, and a POOL's sentence came first wherever its lane ran. Since S7 `brief` walks the graph in the order the flow runs (`_brief_walk`, over `flow_order`, the nodes it cannot reach after them in canvas order), and says each TARGET's "arms" sentence and each POOL's "selects the best of" sentence where the run reaches it (`_block_sentence`), a mosaic's sentences after its block's own, each lane's stages where its first stage runs, and the flow-wide autofocus sentence once, before the first lane, so a flow of one block reads exactly as before (S7, #470). Tonight refuses in words a count that is no count, by validation's own predicate (`_not_a_count`), a stored 0 or negative quota or `perCycle` included, which `max(1, ...)` read as 1, and answers a refused quota `None` (S5, #362); until S7 both UIs read that `None` as 0, "0 cycles each" (#424), and since S7 both campaign cards read it as no count, the #/next card's head line from `campaignHead`, every bar with no maximum and a count with no quota said alone (`memberStatus`), the classic card since the S7 integration (S7, #424). Until S7 Tonight's banked hours and campaign progress were always empty, because the route folded report summaries, which carry no hours per filter (#419, filed in S5). Since S7 the route loads each listed report whole, once for both folds and inside `resolve_tonight`'s worker thread, never on the event loop, so both fold the ledger the archive holds (S7, #419; `test_s7_tonight_route_ledger.py`); the cost grows with the archive. Until S4 a FILTER CYCLE, which carries no goal, had no budget row, so a cycle mosaic, the eighth Example among them, had none, and its hops were priced on no row either (#338). Since S4 every FILTER CYCLE with a slot has one (S4 orchestrator ruling 5): its slot filters joined in `filter` ("L, R, G, B"), `goal_h` None, `tonight_h` the slots' exposures times `cycles` times `per_cycle`, times the live panels for a mosaic, `banked_h` what the ledger holds in those filters, and `strategy` "cycle" with `cycles`, a pool's copies of one cycle making one row. The eighth Example's row is 16 h of shutter across its six panels and, at a measured hop of 160 s, 5.33 h of hops, and its story names no goal, "L, R, G, B cycle: no integration goal (a FILTER CYCLE sets none)", and says what its cycles owe. The Now hub's campaign card read the budget as capture rows that carry a goal (`useCampaign.ts`, a file no S4 task owned), so it took a cycle row's `goal_h` None as 0 and drew a campaign ledger for a flow with no goal at all, "goal 0 hours" and "0.0 of 0 h" (noted on #338). Since the S4 integration the card leaves a row with no goal out of its ledger and its total (`hasGoal`), so a flow whose only rows are cycles is no campaign, as before S4, and beside a capture row that carries a goal a cycle row is not drawn (S4, #406; `nowLedger.test.tsx`). `_campaign` takes per-panel rows from the progress route's answer (`progress`), with no pool required. S3's panel altitudes read the hub's site, not the one `resolve_tonight` is handed (#336). Since S4 `framing._stamp_transit_alt` takes a `site`, and `_mosaic_night` hands it the resolver's, keyword-only and with no default, so no caller reaches the hub's site by leaving it out (S4, #336); the mosaic route still hands it none, since the hub's site is the one that route is about. Both UIs draw the TIMELINE band from the row's `band` (`mosaicBand`, `timelineGeometry`): a hatched rect across the block's window from the best panel's peak down to the worst's, in the block's tone, labelled with the two panels, and a legend entry only when a band is drawn. Since S4 the band's UI test reads `resolve_tonight`'s answer from `fixtures/tonight_mosaic_2x3.json`, which `test_flows_tonight_band_fixture.py` grades against the server, where it embedded a recording nothing re-checked (S4, #353 item 7). The progress chip reads a mosaic in panels (1.2). **Since H4 BUDGET counts this flow's own targets** (H4, #536, H4 orchestrator ruling 6): the route hands `banked_hours_from_reports` the names the flow's run records frames under (`flow_target_names`: a single target and a pool member by name, a mosaic by its live panels, never the bare block name), and each BUDGET row says "banked for these targets" (`_FOR_THESE`), where every report of every flow was folded in. Both folds read the ledger's summaries, a small file every write of a finished report also writes (`_write_summary`, `captures/reports/summaries/<stem>.json`), read once a request on the worker thread (`SessionReporter.summaries`); a report with no usable summary, one finalised before H4 among them, is read whole once and its summary written, and never counts as nothing (`test_h4_budget_for_these_targets.py`, `test_h4_report_summary_cost.py`, `budgetForTheseTargets.test.tsx`). In a flow of several blocks every BUDGET row in a filter still banks the whole flow's hours in that filter (#562). The brief's GUIDE sentence is the rig's, the guider, its settle and the dither the run will use (3.3, #506).

The tests are split by rule, every group case on the clocked simulator through `tests/_group_harness.py`, as in S2. The flows half: `test_flows_vocabulary_s3.py` (the vocabulary), `test_flows_panel_lane.py` (the lane, over `fixtures/panel_lane_cases.json`, which `panelLane.test.ts` grades the editor's mirror against), `test_framing_reframe_carry.py`, `test_framing_angle_budget.py`, `test_framing_route_reframe.py` and `test_flows_identity.py` (the carry, the angle budget and the anchor), `test_flows_compile_entry_s3.py` and `test_flows_to_plan_mosaic.py` (the entry and the expansion), `test_flows_doctor_s3.py` (the doctor), `test_flows_schema_v4.py` and `test_flows_save_rules.py` (the schema and the save), `test_flows_save_switches_counts.py`, `test_flows_rig_facts_route.py`, `test_flows_reframe_end_to_end.py` and `test_flows_continue_skip_param.py` (the routes), `test_engine_measured_cost.py` (the measured cost the routes read), `test_flows_wizard_mosaic.py` and `test_flows_examples_s3.py` (item 4), and `test_flows_tonight_mosaic.py` and `test_flows_progress_mosaic.py` (item 5 and 1.2); in `ui/src`, `panelLane.test.ts`, `flowsSlice.test.ts` and `flowsSaveAnswer.test.ts` (the editor's lane, its move of the loop wire and the save's answer), `flowProgressChip.test.tsx` (the chip), and `tonightTimeline.test.ts` and `tonightMosaicBand.test.ts` (the band). The engine half, S3's rulings and the fixes carried into the slice: `test_group_preslew_floor_wait.py`, `test_group_plain_stop_defers.py`, `test_group_floor_stop_counted.py`, `test_group_jump_leaves_group.py`, `test_group_last_panel_unguided.py`, `test_group_guide_lost_defers.py`, `test_pier_guard_reads_bounded.py`, `test_group_follower_fills_defer_wait.py`, `test_group_pier_state_persisted.py`, `test_resume_arm_recentre_after_flip.py`, `test_resume_arm_run_gating.py`, `test_resume_arm_nothing_tonight_latch.py`, `test_no_light_reference_kind.py`, `test_resume_recover_preflight.py`, `test_plan_identity_group_relations.py`, `test_fits_non_ascii_names.py`, `test_wind_down_guider_stop_before_park.py`, `test_wind_down_abort_during_natural_park.py`, `test_reopen_close_moves_idle_fence.py` and `test_types_mirror_groups.py`; and the harness's own, `test_capture_root_isolated.py` (#309) and `test_group_harness_watchdog.py` (#319). `test_mosaic_spec_claims.py` holds this spec to the code.

Not built in S3: DUSK's `autoResume` and its tooltip, with `FieldDef.help` (#195, which takes FLOW_SCHEMA 5; 3.6), built later in backlog WP-85 (2026-10-07; 3.6, Revision 2, ruling 7); 3.1's `""` missing-key defaults for a TARGET's name, RA and Dec (owner list item 30); the editor surfaces S4 owns, which are `whenWaiting`'s two editors, the card footer, the loop back-arc, the stage-list rail and LOOP PANELS, and #173 and #174; Send to Flow Wizard's stepped sheet and its doors (#196, S4 and S6), so no client sends the wizard's mosaic answers or reads its `notes` yet; a Resume or Recover button that sends `force` (6.3); the Campaign tab's panel rows, which `_campaign` sends and `TonightCampaign.tsx` does not draw; `group_pier` in `ui/src/types.ts` (3.4); #287's backstop in `_capture`, since `plan_identity_errors` refuses a negative panel index on every start path (3.5) and a compile writes none; #308's no-light band checked against real moonless overcast frames (S7), until which S3 orchestrator ruling 8's `RETRY_INTERVAL_S` retry stands; #311's driver-side fixes (#342); a measured hop cost kept across runs, since the engine keeps the last ten from a run's first hop until the next run starts, so each run, and each restart, reads as unmeasured until it makes a hop; and the progress route's use of the panel order (5.2). S3 also found and filed, all open: the golden group trace, which fails at some wall-clock times (#320); Appendix A.1's convergence formula, which weighs only the seams within a row and so under-reports at an angle other than 0, where `framing.convergence_share` weighs both (#321); a jump's visit left out of the pass's counts (#322); a flow's run, a session's Resume and Recover started without the camera-ownership guard, so a run started during a recording slews the mount under it (#323); a panel's cloud-hold floor stop logged as set aside for the run, although the panel is deferred to the next pass (#326, 5.8); ResumeArm reading a pier-guard timeout as a limit refusal (#327); graphs the compile routes accept and `compile_plan` raises on (#328); the #72 recovery count, engine-wide (#329, 5.6); a member's own `after_group`, which the engine does not read (#330, 3.5); the loop wire stranded behind an AUTOFOCUS or GUIDE (#331, 1.5); two wheel slots whose names fold alike writing one `FILTER` (#332); the gallery reading `OBJECT` only (#333); an unvalidated frame type (#334); an all-skipped block's frames counted as dropped (#335, 1.2); the panel altitudes read at the hub's site (#336); no budget row for a cycle mosaic (#338); the brief's grid rows by columns (#339); the auto-reopen close's guider wait and unread park (#343, 6.17); and a refused close's unconfirmed idle stop, which nothing asks again (#345, 6.17). The integration of S3 fixed one more that S3 had filed, START BLANK's SLEW (#340, 1.7), and made both inspectors read `fieldValue` (3.7). It also fixed a stage delete in the classic editor that dropped the flow's settings (#347, 1.6), and filed two questions for the owner: the recount a CONTINUE asks with both totals equal (#348, 5.9), and the level of a pass wire into a one-panel block, a note to the doctor and a loss to Run (#349, 1.4). Since S3, S4 has fixed #320, #321, #322, #323, #326, #327, #329, #330, #331, #332, #333, #334, #335, #336, #338 (under S4 orchestrator ruling 5), #343 and #345, answered #348 and #349 by its orchestrator's rulings 2 and 3 and built both, made the brief, the compile's skip note and the doctor's M6 write a grid's size columns first (#339, S4 orchestrator ruling 1), made #315's last panel the last one the pass can visit (5.6), and fixed #328 in `compile_plan` and validation, filing what lies past the compile as #362 (S4, #189; Revision 8).

### S4: the Target modal and canvas (one shared module, both UIs)

1. `TargetFramingSheet` with every section in 2.4. The local FramingSession.
2. SkyCanvas `panels` and `frameCenter` props; the PanelLayer from server coordinates.
3. `flowsApplyFraming`. `flowsAddNode` returns the id, and dropping a block auto-opens the modal.
4. The Overlay `full` variant; the phone pinned-canvas layout and landscape.
5. The `flowFrame` sheet replacing `flowNode`.
6. The loop back-arc and label chip, the stage-list rail and LOOP PANELS, the card footer and chip.

Tests (tsx scripts exporting `{passed, failed, total}`, with jsdom globals installed before imports):

- Labels sit at the server's coordinates. Mutant "FovOverlay index layout" fails.
- DONE stays locked until the server answers; the offline chip path works.
- One `flowsApplyFraming` per DONE, which means one compile.
- The re-frame confirmation appears only when the progress route reports banked subs, and a skip never asks.
- Any angle is locked on a grid, and Rotate to is locked without a rotator, each with its reason.
- A viewer never sees the altitude column or the night card.
- The back-arc stays below the body cards (a formula test).
- The phone stage list shows the rail, or LOOP PANELS when the wire is absent.
- Opening block A's modal never changes `store.framing`. Mutant "global session" fails.
- Then a probe on the real page with a phone profile (`tools/ui_probe`, visible=true, asserting a view marker), per verify-on-the-real-thing.

**As built** (S4, #189). Items 1 to 6 are built, in four waves, and so is the probe on the real page that the tests end with. This paragraph and the next three say what each wave built, where its tests are and what it filed, the fifth what the third and fourth waves' second runs changed, the sixth what S4 did not build and why, and the last which pins S4 left for the integration to re-pin. The first wave built the foundations of items 2, 3, 4 and 6, the modal's pure model and the readouts its RUN section prints, and the server work S4's rulings and S3's filed issues asked for. It built neither the sheet itself (item 1), which the second wave built, nor the `flowFrame` sheet (item 5), which the third built, nor the real-page probe, which the fourth built (below). Item 2 is `PanelLayer.tsx` and SkyCanvas's `panels`, `panelFov`, `frameCenter`, `onPanelTap` and `onFrameCenterChange` (2.3). Item 3 is `flowsApplyFraming`, and `flowsAddNode` returning the id (2.5); the palette drop that opens the modal came with the third wave. Item 4 is the Overlay's `full` variant (2.2); the phone layouts came with the second wave. Item 6 is the loop back-arc and its chip and the card footer (1.4, 1.2); the stage-list rail and LOOP PANELS came with the second wave. Item 1's pure model is `framingModel.ts` (2.2, 2.4, 2.5), and its RUN numbers are the compile's `readouts` and `rig` (2.4). The server half is 1.4's one-panel pass wire, 3.2's counts, 3.3's completed anchor, rows and read refusal, the all-skipped block's skip (3.4, 3.5, 5.9, 1.2), the rig guard (3.5, 6.3), the simulator's side (5.7), the pause's close-out (5.8), the parks (6.17), and the recount (5.9). Its tests, besides this file: `panelLayer.test.tsx`, `skyCanvasPanels.test.tsx` and `test_framing_panels_fixture.py` (item 2); `flowsApplyFraming.test.ts`, `flowsSlice.test.ts`, `flowsReanchorToast.test.ts`, `countsNotice.test.ts` and `panelLane.test.ts` with `test_flows_panel_lane.py` (item 3, the counts notice and the loop rule); `overlay.test.ts` (item 4); `loopArc.test.ts`, `loopArcDom.test.tsx`, `loopArcNext.test.tsx`, `targetSummary.test.ts` and `cardFooterDom.test.tsx` (item 6); `framingModel.test.ts` with `test_flows_skip_fixture.py` (the model); `test_flows_readouts.py` and `test_flows_compile_payload_docstring.py` (the readouts); `framingCardDial.test.tsx`, `mosaicNightSummary.test.ts` and `test_types_mirror_status.py` (#173, #174); and on the server `test_flows_one_panel_pass_wire.py`, `test_flows_grid_size_words.py`, `test_flows_compile_never_raises.py`, `test_flows_continue_all_skipped.py`, `test_flows_anchor_completion.py`, `test_flows_stored_read_errors.py`, `test_flows_save_answer_fixture.py`, `test_flows_recount_equal_totals.py`, `test_start_paths_refuse_camera_owned.py`, `test_park_readback_async_park.py`, `test_hop_gate_pause_closes_out.py`, `test_reopen_close_guider_bound.py`, `test_refused_close_keeps_idle_stop.py`, `test_zwo_am5_park_noop.py`, `test_sim_pier_side_latched.py`, `test_group_golden_wall_clock.py` and `test_real_config_guard.py`. The suite's config net now moves the profile library as well as the config file (#361), and the wizard and rig-facts route tests isolate the process store through one `isolated_config` fixture (#341). **The fourth wave** (S4, #189) built the probe on the real page that the tests above end with: `tools/ui_probe/routes_s4_frame.json`, with the probe's own cases in `tools/ui_probe/test_probe_s4.py`, run against a local simulator server and never the rig. Two walks reach the one view marker, `data-testid="target-framing-sheet"`, by the clicks a person makes, never by a deep link: the phone's, at 390 x 844 with touch, through the #/next flow list, the stage list, the TARGET's `flowNode` sheet and its FRAME ON SKY row, and the desktop's, at 1440 x 900, through the classic editor's library card, the TARGET card and the inspector's FRAME ON SKY. Each holds DONE locked, with "waiting for the server's panel positions", while the probe holds back the framing route's answer, and open once it lets the answer through; the sky's size, 390 x 390 on the phone and 1080 x 852 beside the 360 px column on the desktop, its canvas 720 x 720; no page wider than the viewport; CANCEL, DONE, MOVE SKY and MOVE GRID each the element found at its own centre; the six section headings; and the PANELS bars' and PEAK cells' text neither cut nor wrapped. Three more phone walks hold the `flowFrame` sheet to the flow `?open=` names: after CANCEL on one flow's framing, a link to another flow's frames the flow it names (FRAME M31, where M33 was open), and a link to a flow that does not exist shows the waiting card with BACK, in `FRAME_OTHER_FLOW`'s words while another flow is open and in `FRAME_FLOW_LOADING`'s on a fresh load with none. Its screenshots show the eighth Example's 3x2 on the sky, its labels 1-1 to 2-3 with their run order, the readout strip and the re-frame line under it, and the sections below: on the phone the sky fills the width above the scroller, and on the desktop it sits beside the column. They also showed four defects, each fixed as it was found (#384): MOVE SKY under SkyCanvas's touch chip, so a finger could not press it (2.2); the PANELS bar's text cut 4 px at its foot; "no peak" wrapped onto two lines in the PEAK column; and the `flowFrame` sheet framing the namesake node of whichever flow was open, which the waiting card now prevents (2.1). The wave's verifier found the card telling a fresh load that another flow was open when none was, and fixed it with `FRAME_FLOW_LOADING`. No survey tile shows in any shot: the simulator has no offline pack and fetches none, so every tile answers 404, which the route file expects. The fourth wave filed #385, the "Your camera" label SkyCanvas pins above a camera outline that panel mode no longer draws, which overprints panel 2-3's label on the phone, and noted on #339 that the night card writes its grid rows by columns.

**The third and fourth waves' second runs** (S4, #189). After the second wave's second verification, the third wave's tasks were run again, each with a verifier, and so was the fourth wave's probe. The third wave's runs made each close the classic host hands the sheet clear only its own opening, the host's half of #382's guard, and wrote down why Escape reaches the #/next sheet's guard only through the modal (2.1). It gave tests that can fail to behaviours that had none: the #/next door on a read-only Example and the overview's default for a flow with no `whenWaiting` or with one this build does not know (#400; 2.1, 1.6); the canvas's key guard under the tablet's edit sheet and palette sheet, and the classic counts line while the flow runs (#401; 2.1, 2.4); and `_live_panels`' member asking, which no clocked night reaches, by a unit case in `test_group_no_guider_reachable_members.py` (5.6 step 7). Its verifiers filed #400 and #401, each a record of the tests they added. The probe's second run found the `flowFrame` sheet's waiting card on the phone's top-left corner, its dashed border on the screen's top and left edges and its width its text's, which no check had graded, and fixed it by giving the sheet its own frame (#384; 2.1); the two waiting walks now carry a `min_inset` the probe grades, and its verifier held each edge on its own (`test_each_edge_is_graded_on_its_own`). Its screenshots, each opened and looked at, showed the modal as the fourth wave's had, with the readout strip, the re-frame line, the six sections' rows and the desktop's column, and three things wrong in files no S4 task owns, which it filed or noted: the sky reading LOADING for good on a rig with no survey source, because `FramingSky.tsx` wired none of SkyCanvas's survey signals (#404); a panel's missing peak altitude explained with a Python class name, "NoSite:", written by `framing._why`, and the eighth Example's camera line saying "bin 1" twice (#405); and the night card's heading written rows by columns in the classic Atlas's `MosaicNight.tsx` as in the modal (on #339).

**Not built in S4**: Send to Flow Wizard's stepped sheet (#196), which S3 item 4 planned to land with the modal, because its stop-condition and auto-resume steps need #191 and #195 first, so it moves to S6 with its doors and no client sends the wizard's mosaic answers yet; the offline re-frame numbers of 2.5, because a client copy of `reframe_carry`'s corner geometry would be a second truth about when counts carry, so offline the move line reads "the server decides at save" (`SERVER_DECIDES`) and only a change of rows or cols asks (`reframeDecision`); #324's shared helper was deferred here: `coords.angular_sep_deg` served callers outside the mosaic that each needed an audit of which way a NaN comparison falls, while every mosaic path already refused a non-finite frame before it measured (`framing._finite`); backlog WP-45 (2026-09-30) later built it, hardening `coords.angular_sep_deg` itself to refuse a non-finite coordinate (it raises `ValueError` rather than clamping a NaN cosine to a separation of 0) and routing hub.py's Sun-exclusion guard and its centring-error check through that same helper in place of a private copy that still clamped; RUN's campaign line for a flow whose automatic resume is off (2.4), because DUSK has no `autoResume` until #195 brings it with FLOW_SCHEMA 5; backlog WP-85 (2026-10-07) later brought the option and the schema, backlog WP-112 (#712) built the Target modal's campaign line for it (`framingApi.campaignLine`), and backlog WP-118 (2026-10-07) built RUN's sentence for it (`runCopy.RESUME_OFF_LINE`, ruling 7), which every RUN surface prints since the wave 16 integration (`copy.notice`); #342's other two suggestions, an unconditional `:Td#` before `:hP#` and a park that takes the pulse lock, because S4 orchestrator ruling 9 asked only for the silent no-op's re-send, so a park lost to a pulse's `:Te#` is still recovered by the driver's second attempt (6.17); and the Campaign tab's panel rows, which `_campaign` sends and neither `TonightCampaign.tsx` nor `TonightCampaignCard.tsx` draws, because they are Tonight's, outside S4's items. Run mode (2.6) and the phone monitor's readouts are S5's.

**Left for the integration** (S4, #406). When S4's record closed, pinned cases in eight files still expected the code as it was before S4, each because an S4 task changed a contract from files that could not reach the pin, and were left red for the integration to re-pin: `test_flows_compile.py`'s two pass-wire cases (#389), `test_flows_graph.py`'s lane bridges (on #331), `test_flows_campaign_brief.py`'s two briefs (#398), `sessionSheets.test.tsx`, which registering `flowFrame` moved, and `r7Parity.test.ts`, which the `flowFrame` sheet's import of `TargetFramingSheetLazy` and the #/next canvas's and inspector's imports of `panelLane`, `targetSummary` and `countsNotice` moved (#400), and three that S4-DOC4's second run filed (#406): `test_flows_routes.py` and `test_flows_rig_facts_route.py`, since the compile answers `readouts` and `rig`, and `authGate.test.ts`, since the store gained `flowsApplyFraming` and `flowsSetSetting`. None is a defect in the code, but until each is re-pinned both suites are red, which can hide a new red among them. The integration re-pinned all eight, each change named in the pin's own docstring or comment as a deliberate pin change with the mutants it was shown red under, and fixed the Now hub's campaign card, which S4 had left reading a cycle row's goal as 0 (S3 item 5, #338).

### S5: run mode, CONTINUE copy, readouts

The modal's run mode fed by `state.group`. The Run button reads from the session (`CONTINUE ... (night n, done/total)`). The phone monitor's readouts, which nothing writes today (`FlowStagesPhoneSheet.tsx` header), are fed from sequence state for mosaics. Tests use recorded state fixtures, and the button copy test compares against the progress route.

**As built** (S5, #189). The three items are built, beside the fixes S4's record and reviews left for the slice, under three rulings the orchestrator made so that it could be built (owner list items 40 to 42). Run mode (2.6): both doors pass `viewOnly` from one reader, `flowRunLive`, and the sheet draws the run's panels from `state.group` through `groupForBlock` and `runPanelsOf`, with a line under each panel the run is shooting or has set aside; its tests are `runMode.test.tsx`, `classicFrameHost.test.tsx` and `flowFrameSheet.test.tsx`. The RUN button's copy (5.9): `runCopy`, CONTINUE with the night and the counts only for a dormant session, and START OVER behind a confirm, in both UIs' headers, toolbars and monitors; its tests are `runCopy.test.ts` and `flowHeaderText.test.ts`. The readouts (5.10): `runReadouts`, fed from the sequence state while the flow's run is live, `hops_costed` rendered as "hops not yet costed", and the phone monitor's tiles among the surfaces fed; its tests are `phoneReadouts.test.tsx` and `flowsDom.test.tsx`. The tests use recorded state fixtures, as the plan asked: `fixtures/sequence_state_mosaic.json`, the states the clocked simulator publishes for a mosaic through a meridian wait, and `fixtures/flow_progress_continue.json`, the progress route's answer for a dormant session of two runs on two nights, each recorded and graded by `test_s5_recorded_state.py` and read by the UI's tests (`flowRunState.test.ts` among them), so the button copy test compares against the progress route's own answer. `flowsApi.ts` and `types.ts` declare what the progress route and the sequence state send, `hops_costed`, `locked_angle` and a nullable `frame_started_at_ms` among them, held to real answers by `test_types_mirror_status.py` (#431).

**Beside the plan, in the engine** (S5, #189). A waiter on its own gating joins the soonest wake at the time its wait really ends (5.1; #380; `test_scheduler_waiter_effective_wake.py`), and a target waiting on its window leaves the night once the mosaic it waits for is set aside (1.6; #374; `test_wait_for_mosaic_skips_waiting.py`, `test_group_member_wait_line_flagged.py`). The frame that fires an instruction is banked before the instruction acts, under S5 orchestrator ruling 3 (5.1; #373; `test_trigger_frame_banked.py`); the reorder also fixed a triggered refocus's cleared baseline, which the soft frame that fired it had re-seeded (#435), and its verifier caught a completion rule's own no-op jump owed again until the jump budget ran out, fixed with `_completion_fired`. The flip gate's retry waits past the crossing and a flip that changed nothing is never called complete, under S5 orchestrator ruling 2 (5.7; #366, #367; `test_flip_retry_margin.py`), and the recovery ladder's sweep counts as tonight's (5.6 step 6, 5.9; #402; `test_resume_recovery_focus_counts.py`). The wind-down and the pause: an Abort in the reopen close completes the stop, a park seen slewing and then unreadable is not taken as parked, and a pause closes out only once its cooler gate, which now watches the weather, has passed (6.17, 5.8; #393, #447, #448; `test_abort_in_reopen_close_completes_stop.py`, `test_park_readback_unreadable_after_slewing.py`, `test_safe_again_after_cooler_gate.py`). The simulator keeps its side through a sync and an unpark, and the clocked harness keeps the meridian cache on the night's clock (5.7; #392, #368; `test_sim_pier_side_latched.py`, `test_group_harness_meridian_clock.py`), whose strict xfail holds #422 until the live chip is cleared.

**Beside the plan, on the server** (S5, #189). The compile routes answer a graph they cannot run in words (3.2; #362; `test_flows_compile_never_raises.py`, `test_compile_route_never_500.py`), a whitespace coordinate is blank on both sides (3.3; #387; `test_flows_typed_coordinates.py`, over `fixtures/typed_coordinates_cases.json`, which `typedCoordinatesFixture.test.ts` reads too), and the doctor reads the vocabulary's `PASS_TYPES` (1.5, 1.8; #375; `test_flows_doctor_pass_types.py`). Tonight's brief names every capture stage, each lane's under its block where there are several, and refuses a count that is no count (section 8's S3; #395, #470, #362; `test_flows_brief_stage_owner.py`, `test_flows_campaign_brief.py`, `test_flows_tonight_counts_finite.py`), joins a list of two with no comma (#407), and leaves "bin 1" off the eighth Example's camera line, which named it twice (#405 item 2); the modal's order is graded against the server's on one table, `fixtures/panel_order_cases.json` (#412 item 1; `test_panel_order_fixture.py`, `panelOrderFixture.test.ts`). The store owns a record's bookkeeping (3.3; #364; `test_persist_flow_bookkeeping.py`), and a plan file the library cannot read is a row with its reason, never a plan left out, and answers 422 `unreadable` at a read or an export (#378; `test_plan_library_unreadable.py`, over `fixtures/plan_list_unreadable.json`). A stored session that fails validation is listed with the step and the rule that failed, a frame type's rule in the store's own words, and one that was armed is warned about once per process (#416; `test_session_unreadable_step_named.py`). A report read that races a snapshot's write is retried, and the read says why a report is missing (`SessionReporter.read`, `scan_reports`); a snapshot built before `finalize` and written after it no longer replaces the final report (#420), and a failed write's warning no longer carries the capture path (#421) (#370; `test_report_load_reason.py`, `test_engine_safety.py`). A master flat's `FILTER` is written by the one writer of the name cards, its key reads the slot as typed, and two slots whose names sanitize alike keep two files (owner list item 28; #371, #372; `test_master_filter_keys.py`, `test_fits_filter_names_distinct.py`). `parse_ra` and `parse_dec` refuse a digit that is not 0 to 9, naming the character (#359; `test_coords_ascii_digits.py`); a panel's missing peak altitude is explained in the exception's own words (#405 item 1; `test_framing_why_copy.py`); and the suite's config net sweeps every path built on the real config directory and watches it with an audit hook (#361; `test_real_config_guard.py`). A shared deploy step writes a failure into the deploy's own log, leaves an unchanged native wheel alone and refuses to install a changed one under a running server (`scripts/deploy_common.ps1`; `test_deploy_common_ps1.py`).

**Beside the plan, in the UI** (S5, #189). The slice reads a param as a number only when it is finite (#358; `coerceParamFinite.test.ts`), a save compiles (1.4; #356; `compileAfterSave.test.ts`), and a newer compile's answer is never replaced by an older one's, nor written for a flow no longer open. The modal: an angle for a grid under S5 orchestrator ruling 1, the single target's RUN, the measured angle's type and the order notes (2.4, 2.5; #411, #413, #408, #412 item 4; `framingModel.test.ts`, `framingSections.test.tsx`, `targetFramingSheet.test.tsx`). The sky: room for panel labels and the degraded state (2.3; #385, #404; `skyCanvasPanels.test.tsx`, `framingSkyDegraded.test.tsx`) and both night cards' headings columns by rows (#339; `mosaicNightHeading.test.tsx`). The loop wire: a stranded wire moved, the classic remove control on the arc, the phone FLOW tab's arc and the footer's reordered words (1.2, 1.4; #410, #355, #360, #357; `panelLane.test.ts`, `targetSummary.test.ts`, `loopArc.test.ts`, `loopArcDom.test.tsx`, `cardFooterDom.test.tsx`, `flowWireDeleteLoop.test.tsx`, with `test_flows_panel_lane.py` on the shared fixture). The Overlay's fraction (2.2; #354; `overlay.test.ts`). The Monitor's last frame: the relay keeps the latest preview when a slow browser's buffer overflows and announces a drop, committed ahead of the slice (#399), and the client re-reads the monitor snapshot on that announcement, at most once each `GAP_RESNAPSHOT_MS`, keeping the snapshot's frame in the store (`rehydrateFromSnapshot`, `useSnapshotPreviewId`), where the #/next Monitor's tile reads it (`wsReconnectPreview.test.ts`, `liveLastFrameReconnect.test.tsx`). An unreadable plan row is drawn as one, with DELETE alone, in the classic library and the #/next NOW list (#378; `planLibraryUnreadable.test.tsx`, `nowUnreadablePlan.test.tsx`). The probe on the real page walked a rotating 2x2 through RUN, the view-only modal and CONTINUE on a phone and a desktop, and Send to Flow Wizard from the Sky and the Atlas, against a local simulator server and never the rig, and fixed what it found in the phone stage sheet's footer, its stage pills, its ADD STAGE and LOG rows and the wizard's title (#463).

**Filed in S5** (S5, #189), those marked fixed being fixed in S5's code: #416, #417, #418, #419, #420 and #421 (both fixed), #422, #423, #424, #425, #426, #427, #428, #429, #430, #431 (fixed), #434, #435 (fixed), #436, #437, #438, #441, #442, #443, #447 and #448 (both fixed), #449, #452, #453, #455, #456, #457, #463 (fixed) and #464 to #469; its verifiers filed #439, #440, #444, #446 and #451, and #446, the claims test that went red once `types.ts` declared `hops_costed`, is answered by 5.10's claim above. This record filed #471, the half of #394 that #394 left open when it closed (6.17), and its verifier filed #472, four claims in 5.6, 5.8 and the owner list that `test_mosaic_spec_claims.py` held only by the shape of the code, each fixed in that file.

**Not built in S5.** Run mode's panel says "shooting now" while the run is paused, holding for cloud or aborting (#451, built in S7, 2.6), and until S7 the sheet let go of run mode for a round trip after a save (#449), because `flowRunLive` read the slice's progress, which a save clears; the run-phase latch still holds STOP after a run ends, since S5 only added the live reader to it (#162); until S7 the loop chip withheld its count by `dirty` (1.4); until S7 `framingModel.ts`'s own `coerceParam` read "Infinity" as a number, where the slice's no longer did, though DONE coerces again through the slice (2.4); `flows.statuses` is still written by nothing, so every stage pill, LED and wire on both canvases' cards reads idle through a live run, while the phone stage list, under S5's rule, shows no status at all while the run is live (#464, deferred under S7 orchestrator ruling 10, section 8's S7); the #/next Plan hub's library still offers LOAD and EXPORT on an unreadable plan (on #378); and until S7 the classic Monitor kept its own copy of the snapshot's frame, which after a server restart could hold a frame from before it (#437).

**The pins S5 moved** (S5, #189). Its own tasks re-pinned what they owned, each named in the pin's docstring or comment as a deliberate change: `test_group_jump_leaves_group.py`'s jumped panel, three frames for its two until #373 and two since; `test_flows_doctor_s3.py`'s M3 sentence for a lane ending on a GUIDE (#375); `test_flows_brief_grid_and_visit.py`'s two skipped panels, "1-3 and 2-3" (#407); and `test_flows_examples_s3.py`'s camera line without "bin 1" (#405 item 2). ### S6: converge the doors

- The Atlas "Send N panels to Plan" / "Add target to Plan" button (`views/AtlasView.tsx:1569-1570`) and the Sky FRAME's forward action become **SEND TO FLOW WIZARD** (Revision 2, ruling 4, #196). The wizard opens pre-filled with the framing, asks what is missing, and writes a TARGET block with the loop wire. The side channel of Plan targets goes (`next/hubs/sky/sheets/quick.tsx:438-466`), and so does the first-run guide's "press Add target to Plan" (`lib/firstRunWizard.ts:77`).
- Delete the synthetic MOSAIC lane card and the false copy: `FramingCard.tsx:37-43`, `quickCopy.ts:225`, `:237-243`, `flowLane.ts:98-117`.
- One overlap constant.

Tests: the Sky and Atlas doors open the wizard pre-filled and produce exactly one TARGET block and no Plan targets, and a grep test asserts the deleted strings ("panels to Plan", "Add target to Plan") are gone.

**As built** (S6, #196). The three items are built, under no ruling of the slice's own. Send to Flow Wizard is one stepped sheet for both UIs, `SendToWizardSheet.tsx` over its pure model `wizardModel.ts`, mounted through its lazy door (`SendToWizardSheetLazy`): the classic Atlas through `SendToWizardHost`, and #/next through a `flowWizard` sheet registered in `wizard/reg.ts`, which the Sky opens with `openFlowWizard`, the prefill travelling in the route so a reload reopens the same wizard. Its steps are TARGET, FRAMING, FILTERS, GUIDING, NIGHT, RESUME and REVIEW (`STEPS`; S6 built the other five, and backlog WP-100 (#196, wave 15) built NIGHT and RESUME, the stop condition and the automatic resume); REVIEW shows the server's compile of the flow it generated, with OPEN IN EDITOR and RUN, and RUN is locked by the compile's own lock first, then by why the rig cannot run it. A door hands it what it holds (`WizardPrefill`): the target's name, blank in free roam so the wizard asks for one, the framing's centre and not the catalogue's, the grid, the overlap in percent and the camera field, and no angle mode, so the wizard asks for it with the PA already typed; the route takes those answers, checked, and the wheel as a rig fact (1.8). A single target, a 1x1 framing or PLAN AS ONE TARGET, is generated as the Deep-sky kind, with no grid keys. Both doors, the Atlas's and the Sky FRAME's, are SEND TO FLOW WIZARD for a mosaic and for a single target, and a mosaic kept on the Sky's quick sheet is offered the same door; the Atlas door is never locked, since the wizard answers missing optics and capabilities itself. The Plan side channel and its copy are gone, the synthetic MOSAIC lane card with them (`flowLane.ts` kept its function as a pass-through that returned the lane unchanged, because `flow.tsx`, which no S6 task owned, still called it, until the integration dropped the call and the function, the #/next Plan banner in `PlanEditor.tsx` with the store slice, action and hook behind it, and the retired door's helpers in `mosaic.ts` (S6, #461)), and the first-run guide says SEND TO FLOW WIZARD. Two copy lines that said the quick flow uses the framing's centre, which it never did, are corrected, and the behaviour is filed (#459); the integration corrected a third, the toast that clearing the Sky's framing raised (`FRAMING_REMOVED`). The overlap is one constant, `lib/framing.ts`'s `DEFAULT_OVERLAP`, which is the server's, read by the store's seed, the Sky's FRAME mode, `next/lib/fov.ts` and the modal (`test_overlap_constant_one.py`). The tests: `test_flows_wizard_door_answers.py` and `test_flows_wizard_route.py` (the route, over `fixtures/wizard_mosaic_answer.json`, a recorded answer both sides read); `wizardModel.test.ts`, `sendToWizardSheet.test.tsx` and `flowWizardSheet.test.tsx` (the sheet and its doors); `doorsConverge.test.tsx` (both doors open the wizard pre-filled), `deletedDoorStrings.test.ts` (the grep; its expiring list of what the doors' task could not reach went with the integration's deletions, and it holds the banner's slice, action, hook and sentence gone too), `mosaicCopyPanelFirst.test.ts` and `quickMosaicDom.test.tsx`.

**Filed in S6, and not built** (S6, #196). Filed: #432 and #433 (the route), #450 (a hub switch in #/next leaves a flow's edits unsaved, and the next open from another hub drops them), #458 (the first-run step cannot tick through the wizard; fixed in the integration: the classic guide counts the store's saved flows and reads the library when it opens, and GENERATE refreshes that library, `firstRunFlowCount.test.tsx`), #459, #460 (the wizard drops a single target's PA without a word when a door sends a PA and no angle mode; fixed in the integration: the one-target note shows for a framed PA and names it, `oneTargetAngleNote`) and #461 (the doors' residuals in files their task did not own); the verifiers filed #445 and #454, each fixed in the working tree. Not built in S6: the stop-condition and auto-resume steps, which waited on #191 and #195 and which backlog WP-100 (#196, wave 15) built as the NIGHT and RESUME steps (`STEPS`); a `whenWaiting` row in the wizard (1.6); and the #/next Sky flow sheet's RUN, which does not print the run copy. The store kept the retired Plan banner's slice, always empty, and `mosaic.ts` kept three Plan helpers with no production caller (`panelsToTargets`, `mosaicGroupId` and `mosaicBaseName`), because files no S6 task owned still imported them, until the integration deleted the slice with its action and hook, and the three helpers, as the paragraph above says (H4, #461). S7 fixed #432 (1.8), #433 and #450, built the second of #459's two shapes, and derived the TARGET node's overlap default from `DEFAULT_OVERLAP` on both sides, where each wrote 25 (#461; 3.1), and the verifier's #445 is re-verified (S7, #432, #433, #450, #459, #445, #461; section 8's S7).

**The pins S6 moved** (S6, #196), in files no S6 task owned, left for the integration (S56-INTEG) to re-pin as deliberate pin changes: `sessionSheets.test.tsx`, which registering `flowWizard` moved; `r7Parity.test.ts`, which the wizard's modules moved; `authGate.test.ts`, whose store actions and slices lost `addTargetsToPlan` and `atlasHandoff`; and the overlap and note pins of `skyCards.test.ts`, `frameDom.test.tsx`, `frameToolsDom.test.tsx` and `frameModel.test.ts`, which the one constant and the reworded note moved.

### S7: validation before any sky, then one supervised night

1. On the simulator, end to end: a 2x2 rotating, a forced solve failure on one panel, a CONTINUE on a second simulated night, and a meridian straddle.
2. Then one supervised rig night on a bright, low-risk 2x1 or 2x2 near the meridian. Measure the hop cost and set the `passes`/`minVisit` defaults from it.
3. Review the durable night log (`captures/logs/<night>.jsonl`), classifying events by their neighbours.
4. File an issue for every human intervention.

Until 2026-09-29 a mosaic had never run on the rig (`docs/reviews/2026-07-30-overnight-systems-test.md:102`; below).

**The supervised night is approved** (owner ruling 8, 2026-09-24). It will be scheduled once S0 to S6 have landed and item 1 is green on the simulator. The night also measures the two first guesses Revision 2 introduced: the re-frame carry threshold (`REFRAME_CARRY_FRACTION`, against the measured centring residual) and the guide-start retry bound.

**As built, item 1** (S7, #189). Item 1 is built, under ten rulings the orchestrator made so that S7 could be built (owner list items 43 to 52, each binding until the owner overturns it), and it is green on the simulator, the gate owner ruling 8 set for the supervised night. The four scenarios are clocked-simulator tests on the group harness, each driven from a real stored flow through `POST /api/flows` and `POST /api/flows/{id}/run`, the compile, the engine and the session, and each grading the ledger, the report, the progress route and the sequence state the UI reads at the same moments (`_flow_night.py`, whose `FlowRig` stops any scenario after `WALL_BUDGET_S`, 180 s of real time, #523): a rotating 2x2 (`test_s7_sim_rotating_2x2.py`), a solve that never lands on one panel, set aside at its third pass while the others complete (`test_s7_sim_solve_failure.py`), a CONTINUE on a second simulated night of that session, a restart later in night 2 continuing night 2's entry (`test_s7_sim_continue_second_night.py`), and a 2x2 whose panels straddle the meridian, one pier change for the group, the pre-flip visits, the wait and the visits after it (`test_s7_sim_meridian_straddle.py`); the straddle leaves `live` out of the viewer's comparison, because a viewer is still served the meridian countdown (#166 item 1, with this evidence added). The probe walked the same four on a private local simulator server, never the rig, from a phone (390x844, touch) and a desktop (1440x900), asserting view markers and that the readouts on the page equal the two routes read before and after them (`routes_s7.json` and `test_probe_s7.py` in `tools/ui_probe`, with `probe.py`'s `readouts` check, `seed_session.py`, which puts a dormant session one night back, and the simulator solver's forced failure, `ASTRODECK_SIM_SOLVE_FAULT`, `test_s7_simsolver_fault.py`). What the probe found is filed below, and #528 is fixed.

**The ledger's cost, measured** (S7, #514, #515, #516). Risk 8 asked for the cost at thousands of frames. At 10 000 frames a banked frame cost about 170 ms of event-loop time before S7, about 330 ms on a camera with pixels, and about 46 ms since, under the 100 ms line the test records; the numbers and what changed are in risk 8, and the budget `test_s7_ledger_cost.py` pins is in A.3.

**Beside item 1** (S7, #189). S7 also built the carry-over the S5 and S6 reviews left, under the rulings, each in the section it changes: the footer fitted (1.2), the chip asking `compiledIsCurrent` and the RUN toggle's opening (1.4), the wizard's unguided cap (1.8), the Overlay callers' fraction (2.2), the sky's legend, degraded copy and square (2.3), the measured angle's age and the modal's finite numbers (2.4), run mode over the known sessions, its set-aside words and order (2.6; the order's test is `panelRowsSetAside.test.ts`, #528), the digit runs and the DUSK offset (3.2), typed coordinates (3.3), a consumed jump's completion and the gate's rise estimate (5.1), the ladder's vouch (5.6), the zero-lead band, the hold's words and the cleared chip (5.7), the waits' weather and the pause's asks (5.8), observing nights, the replay notice and the two-tap arm (5.9), the readouts' reader (5.10), the park's deadline and the leaned stop (6.17), and Tonight's brief, campaign card and ledger (section 8's S3). The #/next Sky finder takes a default site for no coordinates, so it places nothing at 0,0: `useSkyModel` reads a site marked default as none, a row's window is null where it was walked at 0,0, a held lock is placed by the model itself (`place`) or not at all, and a held lock prints nothing it did not compute (`UnplacedTarget`, `lockCardCta`: SET A SITE FIRST, or POSITION HIDDEN FOR THIS ROLE), which also closes the review's IMAGE offered for a lock on a default site (#466); a search pick carries its whole row in the route, so it locks with no ranked list (`lockRowParams`) (#503, #504, #508; `defaultSitePlacement.test.tsx`, `unplacedLock.test.ts`, `searchPickLock.test.tsx`, `targetsDefaultSite.test.tsx`); and with nothing placed, the patch card prints no status word and no count and gives IMAGE THIS PATCH the reason that fits why nothing is placed, and the status row prints no number (`PATCH_NEEDS_SITE`, `PATCH_HIDDEN_FOR_ROLE`, `PatchCard.tsx`, `StatusRow.tsx`; `defaultSitePlacement.test.tsx`, #544, filed by H4 and fixed by its integration). The probe's session seeder accepts only a directory the probe's own server marked as its own (`PROBE_MARKER`, `write_probe_marker`; `test_h4_seed_session_allow_list.py`, #539). The forced solve failure's panel is now set aside for now at the end of its third pass, tried once more when that expires, and set aside for the night after two more misses, where the report marks it skipped (`test_s7_sim_solve_failure.py`, re-pinned, #534). The relay's stalled-loop test now says it protects the harness, not the relay (`test_integration_wss.py`), and `flowRunLive`'s guard against another flow's live run is shown red under its mutant (`flowRunState.test.ts`). The claims of `test_mosaic_spec_claims.py` that went red on H4's code are re-pinned with Revision 11, each with its record of the change.

### S8: gated on #145

- The per-panel convergence correction, `panel PA = block PA + convergence at the panel centre`, in both projections with new golden vectors. The sign comes from the rig measurement.

## 9. Missing features

Tags:

- **ULTRACODE**: build inside the named slice. Tracked together in #189, never filed one by one.
- **FILED #N**: a defect or follow-up found during this design, filed on 2026-09-23 after checking `gh issue list` and scanning each body against the needles file.
- **EXTENDED #N**: a comment with the new evidence on an existing issue rather than a duplicate.

No earlier issue mentioned mosaics. The ones checked were #132, #136, #141, #142 and #145, which are open, and #93, which is closed. The build plan and its order live in the tracking issue, #189.

**Defects (present day unless marked latent)**

| Id | Issue | Gap |
|---|---|---|
| I-01 | FILED #147 (blocks S2) | In accepted mode a visit is not bounded by attempts. The reject branch never increments `taken_this_visit` (`engine.py:3027-3049`, against the comment at `:2862-2866`), so one rejecting filter holds the cycle for up to `max_consecutive_rejects` (10) subs per round, every round. With the step guard off, 20 rejects end the night (`NightQualityStop`). The local counter (`engine.py:2859`) and the `_frames_done` anti-spin (`:2818`, `:4733`) only work because of this defect, so the fix has three parts (5.3). Deterministic. Class: a claim nothing keeps. |
| I-02 | FILED #148 (PLAUSIBLE, unverified on the rig) | The guider is not stood down before an inter-target slew (`engine.py:2314-2509`). Native `start_guiding` is a no-op while its loop lives (`guide/native.py:738-740`), so the pier-side calibration mirror (`native.py:871`) is skipped. Prove it on the simulator first. |
| I-03 | FILED #149 | A drawn flow loop is silently deleted. `flow_order` drops the looped nodes (`compile.py:72-78`), and its docstring says a loop cannot be drawn. `validation_errors` has no cycle check (`flows/models.py:110-171`). The drop resolvers allow the wire (`FlowCanvas.tsx:107-139`). The phone layout strands the nodes (`autoLayout.ts:293-298`). The result is a zero-frame plan with a clean doctor. |
| I-04 | FILED #150 | The TARGET palette default rotation 23.4 commands a connected rotator on every palette, wizard and quick flow (`nodes.py:117`, `nodeDefs.ts:199`; `wizard.py:283-293` overwrites only name and coordinates). The golden fixture pins it (`ngc7331_quick.json:71`). Same class as the rotation-0 migration. |
| I-05 | FILED #151 | Steps are scoped by canvas order, not by wires (`compile.py:233`, `:258`). Which steps a target gets depends on where its card sits, and a target that sorts after every capture is slewed, centred and focused while shooting nothing, silently. S3 scopes every stage by its wires in a graph that holds a multi-panel block (1.5; S3, #151); a graph with none kept the canvas-order rule byte for byte, so there the defect stood. #151's own general case is exactly that residual (two ordinary TARGETs, no mosaic at all), and the W2 backlog (WP-19(a)) closed it: wires now scope every stage in a graph with a multi-panel block **or more than one TARGET/POOL block** (`needs_wire_scoping`, 1.5); only a graph with at most one owner block, mosaic or not, keeps the canvas-order rule byte for byte, so only there does the defect still stand. |
| I-06 | FILED #152 (both UIs) | `flowsConnect` deletes the existing wire on **event** inputs too (`flowsSlice.ts:298-307`), although the server allows event fan-in (`flows/models.py:149-167`) and the campaign example depends on it (`examples.py:200-204`). |
| I-07 | FILED #153 | The flow store silently skips unreadable, unknown-type and future-schema files (`store.py:93-105`). `_migrate` passes any file at version 2 or above through unchanged (`store.py:67-68`), so a future file with only known node types would run with this build's meaning. |
| I-08 | FILED #154 (a claim nothing keeps) | The Sky copy promises that panels cycle every pass (`FramingCard.tsx:37-42`, `quickCopy.ts:237-243`, `flowLane.ts:115`, `next/lib/fov.ts:7`, `:66-69`). The panels go to the classic Plan (`quick.tsx:454-459`), the flow shoots only the centre, and the engine works panel-first (`engine.py:1964-1965`, `:2010`). S6 retired the door the copy described, with its Plan side channel and the synthetic MOSAIC lane card, and a framed mosaic now reaches a flow through Send to Flow Wizard, as a TARGET block whose loop wire rotates its panels (S6, #196; section 8's S6). |
